| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6` | **Project A2A agent cards into library membership facts.** Subsystem: Agent Library projection. Source: an A2A card is an `AgentComponentEntry` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:547`) of kind `AgentComponentKind::A2aAgentCard`, published on the component outbox topic `medulla.agent-component.revision.v1` (`AGENT_COMPONENT_OUTBOX_TOPIC`, `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_component.rs:45`) as an `AgentComponentOutboxEvent` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:1693`). An `AgentLibraryEntry` carries no component kind, capability or agent kind, so it is not the source of these facts: `project_entry` (`crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs`) keeps its current agent mapping (`a :Agent`, `:memberOfLibrary`, `:instanceOfTemplate`) and is not changed by this row. Kind source: one attribute, `agent_kind`, on the card component, whose value is the IRI of an `:AgentKind` individual (for example `https://knuckles-team.github.io/ontology/core#agentKindVerifier`). Add `pub const A2A_AGENT_KIND_ATTRIBUTE: &str = "agent_kind";` beside `PLAYBOOK_TRIGGER_ATTRIBUTE` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`. It is optional: a card without it projects no `:hasAgentKind`. Enforcement at publish (the chokepoint): `validate_draft_kind_body` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/validation.rs` gains the arm `AgentComponentKind::A2aAgentCard => validate_a2a_card_attributes(draft)?`. The new `validate_a2a_card_attributes` refuses `MALFORMED_A2A_AGENT_CARD: a2a agent card '<component_id>' agent_kind '<value>' is not an absolute IRI` when the attribute is present and its trimmed value is empty, contains whitespace, or contains one of the characters `<`, `>`, `"`, `{`, `}`, `\`, `^`, the vertical bar or a backtick, or does not match `<scheme>:<rest>` with `scheme` = an ASCII letter followed by ASCII letters, digits, `+`, `-` or `.`, and `rest` non-empty. It also refuses the same code, naming the capability, for a `declared_capabilities` entry that fails the same IRI rule. Registration: add `MalformedA2aAgentCard => "MALFORMED_A2A_AGENT_CARD"` in alphabetical position to `ServerErrorCode` (`crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs`, beside `MalformedGuardrailRule`), and append `MALFORMED_A2A_AGENT_CARD` to the `AgentComponent` entry of `EXACT_METHOD_ERRORS` in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs` (the list that holds `MALFORMED_GUARDRAIL_RULE`). Mapping: new `pub fn project_component(entry: &AgentComponentEntry) -> Vec<Triple>` and `pub fn component_iri(tenant: &str, component_id: &str) -> String` (`format!("{CORE}component/{}/{}", segment(tenant), segment(component_id))`) in `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs`, beside `project_entry` and reusing its `CORE`, `RDF_TYPE`, `segment`, `term`, `library_iri` and `ntriples`. `project_component` returns no triple unless `entry.lifecycle == AgentLibraryLifecycle::Published` and `entry.kind == AgentComponentKind::A2aAgentCard`; every other kind projects nothing (MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R004.4 and R005.5 add their kinds to the same `match`). For a published card with subject `<component_iri(tenant_id, component_id)>` it returns, in this order: `rdf:type :A2AAgentCard`; `:memberOfLibrary <library_iri(tenant_id)>`; one `:providesCapability <cap>` per `declared_capabilities` entry in stored order, as `NamedNode::new(cap)`; and `:hasAgentKind <kind>` when `attributes["agent_kind"]` is present. A value that `NamedNode::new` refuses cannot be stored (publish refuses it), so the mapping uses `NamedNode::new(..).ok()` and writes no triple for it. Capability strength is not a triple: an A2A card capability stays a claim through the served coverage of MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R126.1, which reads the same component from the Agent Library store. Worker: the component stream is a second consumer of the same Agent Library outbox, committed exactly as R003.6.1 commits entries. In `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs` add `pub const COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE: &str = "agent-component-projection";`, `worker::COMPONENT_CONSUMER: &str = "agent-component-projection"` and `pub fn plan_component_step(entry: &AgentComponentEntry, stored: Option<u64>, previous_entry: Option<&AgentComponentEntry>) -> ProjectionStep`, which applies the rules of `plan_step` with `map::project_component` (a revision not newer than `stored` skips; the stored revision's triples are removed and the new revision's added; a retired or withdrawn revision adds none). `draft_for_step` gains a first argument `source: &str` that replaces the `CURSOR_SOURCE` constant in the envelope id (`<source>:<partition>:<revision>`), the content-version object id (`<source>:<partition>`), the cursor source, the `PolicyRecord.policy_id` (`<source>:<tenant>`); the entry worker passes `CURSOR_SOURCE` and the component worker passes `COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE`; the partition is `cursor_partition(component_id)`. Add `worker::sweep_components` and `worker::run_components`, the mirror of `sweep` and `run`: subscribe `COMPONENT_CONSUMER` to `AGENT_COMPONENT_OUTBOX_TOPIC` (one consumer reads one topic, `MutationKernel::outbox_subscribe`), claim under the same `projection_batch` limit, decode `AgentComponentOutboxEvent` with `medulla_storage::decode_ledger_record`, reject an event that fails `validate()` or names another tenant as `OutboxRejectReason::InvalidEvent`, read the stored offset with `Method::GetChangeCursor { source: COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE, partition, tenant }`, read the previous revision with `AgentLibraryStore::component_revisions(tenant, component_id)`, and commit through `Method::ApplyChangeEnvelopeDraft` under the same service context and graph grant. `spawn` starts both `worker::run` and `worker::run_components`. Writes: the tenant graph, through one governed change envelope per component revision. Refusal: `MALFORMED_A2A_AGENT_CARD` at publish; a row of another tenant is rejected unread. Depends: R002.2, R003.6.1, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R126.1. Caller chain: served `AgentComponent` `Publish` (`Method::AgentComponent { op: AgentComponentOp::Publish }`) → `AgentComponentDraft::validate` → `validate_draft_kind_body` → `validate_a2a_card_attributes`; then `src/main.rs` bootstrap → `medulla_h_admin::agent_library_projection::spawn` → `worker::run_components` → `sweep_components` → `plan_component_step` → `map::project_component` → `draft_for_step(COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE, ..)` → `Method::ApplyChangeEnvelopeDraft`. Shape: `project_component` and `component_iri` in `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs`; `plan_component_step`, `COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE` and the `source` argument of `draft_for_step` in `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs`; `sweep_components`, `run_components` and `COMPONENT_CONSUMER` in its `worker` module; `validate_a2a_card_attributes` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/validation.rs`; no second mapping table, no second commit route and no new method. Budget: linear in declared capabilities, at most 64 per component (`MAX_CAPABILITIES`); benchmark `crates/medulla-h-admin/benches/agent_library_projection.rs` (new, beside `ontology_diff.rs`). | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs::tests::a2a_card_maps_to_member_with_capabilities_and_kind` (L1) asserts the four triple forms in order, no triple for a retired card or a `Tool` component, and no `:hasAgentKind` without the attribute. Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs::tests::component_step_replays_replaces_and_retires` (L1) asserts the skip, replace and retire steps and that the draft names `agent-component-projection`. Test: `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/validation.rs::tests::a2a_card_with_non_iri_kind_is_refused` (L1). Test: `crates/medulla-server-served-tests/src/split_tests/medulla_h_admin_agent_component_projection_tests.rs::publish_projects_membership_and_a2a_capabilities` (planned) (L3). The proof level is the Rust served harness, not a Python session test, because a Python test would have to wait for the background worker's timer; the harness runs one sweep itself and adds no route. Worked example, in order: (1) publish an `A2aAgentCard` component with `declared_capabilities` of two absolute IRIs and `agent_kind` an absolute IRI through the served `Method::AgentComponent { op: AgentComponentOp::Publish }` as tenant T; (2) call `medulla_h_admin::agent_library_projection::worker::sweep_components(&state)` once (it claims the outbox row, plans the step and commits `Method::ApplyChangeEnvelopeDraft`, as above) and assert it returns one committed step; (3) read back through the served `Method::Sparql` with `SELECT ?cap WHERE { <component_iri(T, id)> <CORE#providesCapability> ?cap } ORDER BY ?cap` and assert both capability IRIs, then `ASK { <component_iri> a <CORE#A2AAgentCard> }` is true and a read as tenant U returns no row; (4) call `sweep_components` again and assert it commits nothing and the SELECT is unchanged (replay); (5) publish a bare-name capability and assert the publish refuses `MALFORMED_A2A_AGENT_CARD`. The L4 Python restart test belongs to R003.6.1 only. Note for existing data: a card whose `declared_capabilities` are bare names is refused at publish as stated above; pack import maps `a2a-card://` entries through the same `validate_a2a_card_attributes`, so no bare-name card is stored. |
Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R126.1, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R002.2, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6.1
| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6.1` | **Run the Agent Library projection worker.** Subsystem: server bootstrap. Code: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs` with `spawn`, `sweep` and `CONSUMER`, patterned on `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/connector_pack_projection.rs`. The worker reads outbox rows in order and calls `project_entry`. Design: the Agent Library outbox and the tenant graph are two durability domains, and one admitted batch commits one domain. The worker therefore commits the triples and the consumer offset together in the graph domain: one governed change envelope (`ApplyChangeEnvelopeDraft`, the dispatch, authorization, durability and audit of any caller's write) whose graph operations are `RemoveTriples` of the revision the offset names, then `AddTriples` of the new revision, and whose change cursor is the consumer offset itself: source `agent-library-projection`, partition the hex SHA-256 of the agent id, position the entry revision, and `expected_previous` the stored offset, so a concurrent double write refuses. The envelope's idempotency key names the consumer, tenant, agent and revision. The Agent Library lease is acknowledged on the Agent Library's own handle only after that commit; a crash in between redelivers the row, which the offset then skips. No second route and no cross-domain batch exist. A replay of one row writes no new triple: a row whose revision is not newer than the stored offset is acknowledged without a commit. A newer entry revision replaces the triples of the older revision. A retired entry removes its triples. Config: `SchedulerConfig.orchestration_limits.projection_batch` (`crates/medulla-work-types/src/scheduler_config.rs`), default 100, range [1, 1000], rows. Refusal: a row from another tenant is never read. Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R019. Caller chain: `src/main.rs` bootstrap (`src/main.rs:488`) → new `medulla_h_admin::agent_library_projection::spawn(state)` on the next line → `worker::run` → `sweep`, patterned on `worker::sweep` of `connector_pack_projection` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/connector_pack_projection.rs:97`), which claims outbox rows under the `CONSUMER` lease. Shape: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs` (module `worker` with `run`, `sweep`, `CONSUMER`; the pure `plan_step` and `draft_for_step` beside it) beside `connector_pack_projection.rs`; it reuses `AgentLibraryStore` and `AgentLibraryStateExt` of `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_library.rs`, `MutationOutboxLease` and the outbox claim types of `medulla_transaction`, and adds one `orchestration_limits` field to `SchedulerConfig` (`crates/medulla-work-types/src/scheduler_config.rs:30`). | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs::tests::replay_writes_nothing_newer_replaces_and_retire_removes` (L1). Test: `crates/medulla-work-types/src/scheduler_config.rs::tests::projection_batch_defaults_to_100_within_1_to_1000` (L1). Test: `tests/orchestration/test_agent_library_projection_served.py::test_projection_replays_and_survives_restart` (planned) (L4) publishes three entries, restarts the engine and asserts no duplicate triple. |
Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R019

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R004.4` | **Project `GuardrailRule` components as best practices.** Subsystem: Agent Library projection. Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6.1, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1. Build order: after R003.6, which adds `pub fn project_component(entry: &AgentComponentEntry) -> Vec<Triple>` and `pub fn component_iri(tenant: &str, component_id: &str) -> String` in `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs`, and `plan_component_step`, `COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE`, `worker::sweep_components` and `worker::run_components` in `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs`; this row adds one arm to the `match entry.kind` of `project_component`. Source: a stored `AgentComponentEntry` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:547`) with `kind == AgentComponentKind::GuardrailRule` and `lifecycle == AgentLibraryLifecycle::Published`. The rule lives in two attributes, `GUARDRAIL_APPLIES_TO_CLASS_ATTRIBUTE` and `GUARDRAIL_SOURCE_GENERATION_ATTRIBUTE`, decoded by `GuardrailRule::from_attributes(&entry.component_id, &entry.attributes)` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_guardrail.rs:215`). Enforcement: publish is the chokepoint. `validate_draft_kind_body` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/validation.rs`) already refuses a rule whose `applies_to_class` is missing, blank or not an IRI with `MALFORMED_GUARDRAIL_RULE`, so every stored rule decodes. The projection therefore has no refusal and records no sweep code; no `GUARDRAIL_SCOPE_MISSING` code is added. Mapping: new `fn project_guardrail_rule(subject: &NamedNode, entry: &AgentComponentEntry) -> Vec<Triple>` in `map.rs`, called from the arm `AgentComponentKind::GuardrailRule => project_guardrail_rule(&subject, entry)`, where `subject` is `NamedNode::new_unchecked(component_iri(&entry.tenant_id, &entry.component_id))`. It returns, in this order: `rdf:type :BestPractice`; `:memberOfLibrary <library_iri(tenant_id)>`; `:appliesToTaskClass <applies_to_class>` built with `NamedNode::new(..)`. When `from_attributes` fails (unreachable after publish) or `NamedNode::new` refuses the class it returns no triple. The source generation stays on the component and is read through `AgentComponent` `Current`; it is not projected. `:BestPractice` and `:appliesToTaskClass` are already declared in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/capability-v1.ttl`, so no vocabulary is added. Writes: the tenant graph, through the R003.6 component stream: one `ApplyChangeEnvelopeDraft` per component revision under `COMPONENT_CURSOR_SOURCE`; a retired or withdrawn rule removes its triples by the R003.6 step rules. Caller chain: `src/main.rs` bootstrap → `medulla_h_admin::agent_library_projection::spawn` → `worker::run_components` → `sweep_components` → `plan_component_step` → `map::project_component` → `project_guardrail_rule`. Shape: one function and one match arm in `map.rs`; no new method, code, store, constant or consumer. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection/map.rs::tests::guardrail_rule_maps_to_best_practice_with_scope` (L1) asserts the three triples in order for a published rule, none for a retired rule, and none for a `Tool` component. Test: `tests/orchestration/test_agent_library_projection_served.py::test_guardrail_rule_projects_applies_to_task_class` (L4) publishes a `guardrail_rule` component through `AgentComponent` `Publish` and reads `:appliesToTaskClass` back through `Method::Uql`. The projection is asynchronous (the worker of R003.6.1 sweeps the outbox), so the test waits with a bounded poll: repeat the UQL read every 0.25 s until it returns the triple or 30 s pass with `time.monotonic()`, then fail with the last rows read; it never sleeps a fixed time and never reads the outbox. |
Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6.1

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R005.5` | **Project `model_profile` components.** Subsystem: Agent Library projection. Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6.1. Source: a stored `AgentComponentEntry` with `kind == AgentComponentKind::ModelProfile` and `lifecycle == AgentLibraryLifecycle::Published`. The window is the typed fact `AgentComponentFacts::ModelProfile { context_window_tokens: u32, .. }` (`crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs:335`), not an attribute. Enforcement: publish is the chokepoint. `validate_window` (`facts.rs:529`) refuses a zero window (`agent component context_window_tokens must be non-zero`), so every stored profile has a window. The projection has no refusal and records no sweep code; no `MODEL_PROFILE_WINDOW_MISSING` code is added. Mapping: new `fn project_model_profile(subject: &NamedNode, entry: &AgentComponentEntry) -> Vec<Triple>` in `map.rs`, called from the arm `AgentComponentKind::ModelProfile => project_model_profile(&subject, entry)` of `project_component`, with `subject = component_iri(tenant_id, component_id)`. It returns, in this order: `rdf:type :ModelProfile`; `:memberOfLibrary <library_iri(tenant_id)>`; `:contextWindow` as the typed literal `Literal::new_typed_literal(context_window_tokens.to_string(), xsd:integer)`. Facts of another variant (unreachable, publish binds facts to kind) return no triple. `:ModelProfile` and `:contextWindow` are declared in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/orchestration-v1.ttl`. The `HarnessConfig` family keeps reading the window from the component head, not from the graph (MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R128.5, R128.8); the triple serves graph queries only (Q5, R005.6). Writes: the tenant graph through the R003.6 component stream. Mapping: new `pub fn project_graph(entry: &AgentGraphEntry) -> Vec<Triple>` in `map.rs`: no triple unless `entry.lifecycle == AgentLibraryLifecycle::Published`, else `topology::project_topology(&graph_iri(&entry.tenant_id, &entry.graph_id), entry)` (which already returns nothing for `topology == None`). Worker: one generic stream, so the graph stream is not a third hand copy. In `crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/agent_library_projection.rs` add `pub(crate) trait ProjectedStream { type Entry; type Event: serde::de::DeserializeOwned; const SOURCE: &'static str; const TOPIC: &'static str; fn entry(event: &Self::Event) -> &Self::Entry; fn validate(event: &Self::Event) -> Result<(), String>; fn tenant(entry: &Self::Entry) -> &str; fn record_id(entry: &Self::Entry) -> &str; fn revision(entry: &Self::Entry) -> u64; fn plan(event: &Self::Event, stored: Option<u64>, previous: Option<&Self::Entry>) -> ProjectionStep; fn revisions(store: &AgentLibraryStore, tenant: &str, record_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<Self::Entry>, String>; }` and `worker::sweep_stream<S: ProjectedStream>(state, subscribed) -> Result<bool, String>` and `worker::run_stream<S>(state)`, the body of today's `sweep`/`run` with `CONSUMER`, `CURSOR_SOURCE`, `AGENT_LIBRARY_OUTBOX_TOPIC`, `AgentLibraryOutboxEvent`, `plan_step` and `store.revisions` replaced by the trait items; the consumer name, cursor source, envelope id prefix, content-version id and `PolicyRecord.policy_id` all use `S::SOURCE` through the `source` argument that R003.6 adds to `draft_for_step`. Three impls: `EntryStream` (`SOURCE = "agent-library-projection"`, today's entry stream, `plan` = `plan_step`), `ComponentStream` (R003.6, `SOURCE = "agent-component-projection"`) and `GraphStream` (`SOURCE = "agent-graph-projection"`, `TOPIC = AGENT_GRAPH_OUTBOX_TOPIC`, `Event = AgentGraphOutboxEvent`, `record_id = graph_id`, `revisions = AgentLibraryStore::graph_revisions`, `plan` = new `pub fn plan_graph_step(kind: AgentGraphMutationKind, entry: &AgentGraphEntry, stored: Option<u64>, previous_entry: Option<&AgentGraphEntry>) -> ProjectionStep`, the rules of `plan_step` with `map::project_graph`: a revision not newer than `stored` skips, the stored revision's triples are removed and a `Publish` revision's added, a `Retire` adds none). `sweep`, `run`, `sweep_components` and `run_components` are deleted in favour of `sweep_stream::<EntryStream>` and so on; `spawn` starts `run_stream::<EntryStream>`, `run_stream::<ComponentStream>` and `run_stream::<GraphStream>`. The cursor partition is `cursor_partition(graph_id)`; an event that fails `validate()` or names another tenant is rejected unread as `OutboxRejectReason::InvalidEvent`. Add the field `DecisionInputs.steps: BoundedVec<AssemblyStep, 64>` with `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BoundedVec::is_empty")]`. A request with no steps keeps its exact record digest. Read: new `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/steps_read.rs` with `read(state, graph, verified, tasks) -> Result<BoundedVec<AssemblyStep, 64>, String>`, beside `topology_read.rs`. The triple-to-`Fact` rendering inside `stat_usage_guidance::with_usage_guidance_runtime` moves to a new `stat_usage_guidance::facts_of_triples`, and both readers call it. For each IRI of `request.requirements.tasks`, the read takes the core `:hasStep` objects, and per step its `:precedes` and `:requiresCapability` objects. A task with no step adds no `AssemblyStep`; its class closure is unchanged. Order: `medulla_decision_stats::decision::usage_guidance::order_steps` with an empty rate map, the one step-ordering routine. `position` is the index in that order. Refusals: `OrderError::Cyclic` refuses `STEP_ORDER_CYCLE: <step ids>`. `OrderError::DuplicateStep`, more than 64 steps, or more than 16 capabilities on one step refuse `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID`. Registration: add `StepOrderCycle => "STEP_ORDER_CYCLE"` to `DecisionErrorCode` in `crates/medulla-decision-types/src/decision/errors.rs`; `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/error_registry.rs` declares it through `DecisionErrorCode::ALL`. Append `STEP_ORDER_CYCLE` to the `AgentAssemble` entry of `EXACT_METHOD_ERRORS` in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Solver: the 0-1 programme, objective and search are unchanged. Only the requirement derivation reads the steps. `required_capabilities(requirements, steps, ontology_digest)` in `crates/medulla-decision-outcome-types/src/decision/derivation.rs` gains the `steps: &[AssemblyStep]` argument. Each step capability is required with premise `fact: "required_by_step:<step>"`, class `Definition` and provenance `Request { field: "steps" }`. A step capability that fails `agent_ontology::is_capability` abstains with the existing `UnresolvedCapabilityIri`. Both callers change in the same commit: `crates/medulla-compute-assemble/src/assemble/conclude.rs:98` passes `&inputs.steps`, and `capability_coverage_query` (`derivation.rs:468`) passes an empty slice. `:needsSkill` is not read: a skill IRI is not a capability IRI on main. Writes: none. Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R036. Replay reads the recorded `inputs.steps`, so a replayed record is byte-identical. | `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R007.4` | **Enforce slot agent-kind constraints before selection.** Subsystem: `AgentAssemble`, stage 2 Candidates. A slot with `swarm:requiresAgentKind` accepts only visible candidates whose component carries that kind: the `agent_kind` attribute (`A2A_AGENT_KIND_ATTRIBUTE`, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6) of the candidate's `AgentComponentEntry`, compared as an exact IRI. A candidate without the attribute matches no constrained slot. The slot kind is `SlotTopology.required_agent_kind` (MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R006.5) of the request's `TemplateFacts.topology`. An unconstrained slot keeps current behaviour. Build order: after R003.6 (adds `A2A_AGENT_KIND_ATTRIBUTE`; not landed); R006.5 is landed, so `SlotTopology.required_agent_kind` exists. `assemble_for` reads its candidates through `store.assembly_candidates` (not `library_candidates`) and the request's templates through `store.assembly_template`; after both reads, in its Constraints stage, it collects the `topology.slots` of every template and calls `filter_by_slot_kind(&slots, &entries, &views)` itself, then keeps only the entries whose view survived. A view survives when some slot accepts it: an unconstrained slot accepts every candidate, a constrained slot only its kind; per-slot assignment is not enforced here (the solver assigns). Kinds are data and there is no term list in the solver. When a constrained slot has no visible candidate of its kind, the refusal is returned as an error string `NO_CANDIDATE_OF_KIND: slot '<node_id>' requires agent kind '<iri>' and no visible candidate carries it` (the `Display` of `AssembleRefusal`), like `STEP_ORDER_CYCLE`: `assemble_for` has no abstain path before the solver, so no sealed `DecisionRecord` abstention is produced for it. Writes: none. Depends: R006.5, R003.6. Caller chain: served `AgentAssemble` → `handle_agent_assemble` → `assemble_for` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/assemble.rs:46`) → `store.assembly_candidates` and `store.assembly_template` → `filter_by_slot_kind` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/candidates.rs:207`). Shape: `filter_by_slot_kind(slots, entries, views) -> Result<Vec<CandidateView>, AssembleRefusal>` in `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/candidates.rs` beside `view_of`; it reads `entry.attributes[A2A_AGENT_KIND_ATTRIBUTE]` from the `entries` that `assemble_for` already holds, `views[i]` being the view of `entries[i]` (no graph read and no new `CandidateView` field) and `required_agent_kind` from the slot facts, with no term list in the solver. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/stat_tests/assemble_vocabulary_tests.rs::assemble_enforces_slot_agent_kind` (L3). It excludes an executor-only candidate from a verifier slot, accepts a verifier and asserts the `NO_CANDIDATE_OF_KIND:` error string when none is visible. |
Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R006.5, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R003.6

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R010.6` | **Evidence query for the best configuration per solved task.** Subsystem: harness metrics. One UQL statement over the caller's `RunTrace` nodes with its own pure function, because its rows carry trace ids, which the numeric `MetricRow` table cannot. Types in `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/harness_metrics.rs`: `pub const HARNESS_CONFIG_EVIDENCE: &str = "harness_config_evidence";`, `pub const CONFIG_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS: [&str; 3] = ["cost_per_solved_task", "solved", "total"];`, `pub const DEFAULT_MIN_TRACES_PER_CONFIG: u32 = 20;`, `pub const MIN_TRACES_PER_CONFIG_BOUNDS: (u32, u32) = (5, 1_000);`. `ConfigEvidenceQuery { model_ref: String, task_class: String, min_traces: u32 }` with `from_params(text: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String>, number: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<f64>) -> Result<Self, String>` reading the UQL parameters `$model_ref` and `$task_class` (required, non-empty after trim, else `INVALID_ARGUMENT: $<name> is required`) and `$min_traces` (optional, default `DEFAULT_MIN_TRACES_PER_CONFIG`; a non-integer or a value outside the bounds refuses `INVALID_ARGUMENT: $min_traces must be an integer in [5, 1000]`). The threshold is a per-read parameter, not a stored knob: the statement writes nothing and decides nothing. `TraceSufficiency { Sufficient, Insufficient }`, a `Copy` classification set once by the constructor, so it gets no machine. `ConfigEvidence { config: String, cost_per_solved_task: Option<f64>, solved: u64, total: u64, trace_ids: Vec<String>, sufficiency: TraceSufficiency, abstained: Option<&'static str> }`. Behaviour: `pub fn harness_config_evidence(query: &ConfigEvidenceQuery, traces: &[(String, &Map<String, Value>)]) -> Vec<ConfigEvidence>` over `(node id, properties)` pairs. A trace is in scope when its `runModel` equals `model_ref`, its `runTaskClass` equals `task_class` and it has an `appliedHarnessConfig` text; others are ignored. Groups are keyed by `appliedHarnessConfig` in `BTreeMap` order. Per group: `total` is the member count; `solved` counts `status == "completed"`; `trace_ids` are the member node ids sorted ascending; `sufficiency` is `Insufficient` when `total < min_traces`, and the group stays in the result; cost: `solved == 0` gives `None` with `abstained = Some(NO_SOLVED_TRACE)`; any member without a finite `costUsd` gives `None` with `abstained = Some(PRICE_UNKNOWN)` (the existing constant); else the sum of `costUsd` over all members divided by `solved`, the formula of `harness_cost_per_solved_task` (R010.2). Wire: add `HarnessQuery { name: HARNESS_CONFIG_EVIDENCE, groups: &[], columns: &CONFIG_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS }` to `HARNESS_QUERIES`. In `crates/medulla-h-query-read/src/query/harness_metrics.rs`, `handle_harness_metric` returns `handle_harness_config_evidence(ctx, query, params)` for that name, the mirror of `handle_evolution_trace_metrics`: decode with `text_param` and `number_param`, read one `sql_read_snapshot`, collect `(node id, node_row_object)` for nodes with `node_type == "RunTrace"`, call `harness_config_evidence`, and answer one `UqlResult::Rows` with columns `CONFIG_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS`: one row per group, `id = config`, channels `[cost_per_solved_task, Some(solved), Some(total)]`, provenance `trace_ids` = the JSON array text of `trace_ids` (`serde_json::to_string`) and `sufficiency` = `sufficient` or `insufficient`; each `abstained` group adds the warning `abstained <code>: <config>` as the other harness statements do. A refusal returns `Response::err(ctx.req_id, refusal)`; `INVALID_ARGUMENT` is a shared code, so nothing is registered. | `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R011.10` | **Apply the evaluation result once through the `ModelProfile` publish.** Subsystem: governed write. Language: Python, hand-written over the generated models. Helper: `async def record_measured_fact(client, model_profile_ref: str, field: Literal["shell_proficiency", "stopping_behaviour", "context_window_tokens"], value: Union[str, int], evaluation_id: str, digest: str) -> MeasuredFactRecorded` in `medulla/decision_client.py`, with the frozen dataclass `MeasuredFactRecorded { component_id: str, entry_revision: int, replayed: bool }` beside it. Steps: (1) read the head with `send_agent_component(client, {"op": {"op": "current", "tenant_id": client.tenant, "component_id": model_profile_ref}})` (`medulla/generated/storage.py:477`); no head, or a head whose `kind` is not `model_profile`, raises `ValueError("MODEL_PROFILE_UNKNOWN: <ref>")` before any write. (2) Build the next draft as the copy of every `AgentComponentDraft` field of the head entry (the Python mirror of `AgentComponentEntry::as_draft`, `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:809`) with one change in `facts` (variant `model_profile`): `shell_proficiency` or `stopping_behaviour` set to `MeasuredFact { value, evaluation_run_ref: ObservationRef { evaluation_id, digest } }`, or `context_window_tokens` set to the integer `value`. (3) Replay check: when the head already holds exactly that field value (for a measured fact the same `value`, `evaluation_id` and `digest`), return `MeasuredFactRecorded(component_id, head.entry_revision, replayed=True)` and send nothing; this is how a retried evaluation result adds no revision. (4) Publish once with `{"op": {"op": "publish", "request": {"context": ctx, "component": draft}}}`, where `ctx` is an `AgentLibraryMutationContext` whose `tenant_id` is `client.tenant`, whose `expected_revision` is `head.entry_revision`, and whose other fields are the empty placeholders the server replaces: `bind_agent_library_context` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_library/context.rs:9`) binds principal, caller principal, attempt nonce, actor scope, purpose, policy revision, policy digest, policy decision id, idempotency key and creation time from the verified request, never from the body. The transport idempotency key is `measured-fact:<model_profile_ref>:<field>:<evaluation_id>`. A concurrent head move fails the `expected_revision` compare-and-set and the helper returns that refusal unchanged; a publish whose measured fact has no valid run reference is refused by `MeasuredFact::validate` (MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R048.2) and also returned unchanged. On success it returns `MeasuredFactRecorded(component_id, result.entry.entry_revision, replayed=False)`. No promotion call and no pointer move follow: the published head is the current fact. Writes: one `ModelProfile` component revision. Consumer: the evaluation jobs of MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE and the proof of R011.11. Depends: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R048.2. Caller chain: an evaluation job → `record_measured_fact` → `Method::AgentComponent` `Current` → `Method::AgentComponent` `Publish` → `component_publish` (`crates/medulla-h-admin-schema/src/admin/agent.rs:174`) → `bind_agent_library_context` → `AgentComponentDraft::validate` → `AgentLibraryStore::publish_component`. Shape: one helper and one result dataclass in `medulla/decision_client.py`; no server method and no Rust code. | Test: `tests/orchestration/test_measured_fact_served.py::test_evaluation_result_publishes_one_profile_revision` (L4) publishes a profile, records `shell_proficiency`, reads the new head through `Current` and asserts the fact and revision 2, asserts that a repeat returns `replayed=True` with revision 2 and no revision 3, and asserts that an empty `evaluation_id` is refused by the engine. |
Depends: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R048.2

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.2` | **Behaviour: classify a spec file by named rule.** Subsystem: spec indexing. Code: `classify_spec_file(relative_path, front_matter) -> Result<SpecNodeKind, SpecFolderRefusal>` in `crates/medulla-core/src/spec_folder.rs`. `front_matter` is the flat `key: value` block between a leading `---` line and the next `---` line, as `BTreeMap<String, String>` (at most 64 keys, at most 4096 bytes; a block that is malformed or over a bound yields an empty map, never a refusal). `relative_path` is `/`-separated and relative to the repository root. Rules are the named constants `DEFAULT_SPEC_FILE_RULES`, applied in this order, first match wins: (1) `front-matter-kind`: the key `kind` holds one of `task`, `goal`, `document`, `memory`, `policy` and maps to that kind; (2) `constitution-file`: the file name is exactly `constitution.md` and maps to policy; (3) `tasks-file`: the file name is exactly `tasks.md` and maps to task; (4) `goal-front-matter`: the key `goal` holds a nonempty value and maps to goal; (5) `memory-folder`: a path segment other than the file name equals `memory`, the extension is `.md`, and it maps to memory; (6) `spec-document`: the file name is one of `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `research.md`, `quickstart.md`, `data-model.md`, `requirements.md` and maps to document. Each rule has the stable name above, stored as `:classifiedByRule`. Refusal: `SpecFolderRefusal::UnknownKind`. Writes: none. Depends: R012.1. Caller chain: `project_spec_artifacts` (`MEDULLA-SDLC-R014.2`) → `classify_spec_files` (R012.4) → `classify_spec_file`. Shape: `classify_spec_file` and `DEFAULT_SPEC_FILE_RULES` in `crates/medulla-core/src/spec_folder.rs`; the function is pure and depends on `SpecNodeKind` of R012.1 only, so `medulla-server-graph` calls it without a sibling handler crate. | Test: `crates/medulla-core/tests/spec_folder_mapping.rs::default_rules_classify_each_file_kind` (L1). |
Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.1

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.4` | **Classification step of the spec projection, and the refusal for an unknown file.** Subsystem: spec indexing. `project_spec_artifacts` (`MEDULLA-SDLC-R014.2`) is the one route that lowers spec files; this row adds a classification step to it and no second route. New function `pub fn classify_spec_files(repo: &str, files: &[(String, Vec<u8>)]) -> ClassifiedSpecFiles` in `crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion/spec_projection.rs`, with `pub struct ClassifiedSpecFiles { pub operations: Vec<serde_json::Value>, pub unknown: Vec<String> }`. It considers each `.md` file of the batch whose path satisfies `is_spec_artifact` or has a path segment `.specify` (a path repeated in `files` is read once, at its first occurrence; a file that is not UTF-8 is skipped). For each file it calls `parse_front_matter(text)` and `classify_spec_file_by_rule(path, &front_matter)` (`crates/medulla-core/src/spec_folder.rs`, R012.2) and writes one `upsert_node` with id `speckind:<repo>:<path>`, `type` = `SpecNodeKind::class_name()` (`SpecTask`, `SpecGoal`, `SpecMemory`, `SpecPolicy`, or `Specification` for a document), and string properties `repo`, `path`, `classifiedByRule` (the rule name) and `blob_digest` (`digest_of(text)`, the `sha256:` form of the file bytes). `project_spec_artifacts` calls it after the artifact re-ingest and appends the operations to `result` through the existing `index_node` and `index_edge` loop, so they commit in the same envelope. Refusal: `classify_spec_file_by_rule` returns `SpecFolderRefusal::UnknownKind` for a file that matches no rule. The file is then written as a plain node of `type` `Document` with `repo`, `path` and `blob_digest` and no kind and no `classifiedByRule`, its path is added to `unknown`, and `project_spec_artifacts` appends `IndexDiagnostic { code: "SPEC_FILE_UNKNOWN_KIND", message: <path> }` to the `diagnostics` of that file's entry in `result.file_outcomes` (`IndexFileOutcome`, `crates/medulla-ingestion-types/src/ingestion_wire.rs:42`; the entry is looked up by `file_path` and is skipped when the vector is full or no entry exists; the `verdict` does not change). The batch continues. Depends: R012.2. Test: `tests/orchestration/test_spec_folder_served.py::test_unknown_file_becomes_plain_document` (L4). |

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R004.6.3` | **Query by task class with exact or descendant match.** Params (the `UqlParams` of the statement): `task_class` (text, required, a task-class node id), `match_mode` (text, `exact` or `descendant`, default `exact`; any other value refuses `PARAMETER_INVALID`), `taxonomy_revision` (number, optional, whole, non-negative), `page_size` (number, optional, held to `[1, orchestration_limits.page_size_max]` like `RunTraceUsageQuery::from_params`) and `cursor` (text, optional). Authorization runs before rows, counts and pagination: the statement reads only the caller's RLS-filtered snapshot, so a foreign tenant sees no rows and no count. Taxonomy and closure: a task class is a graph node (the `task_class_ref` that R004.6.2 checks with `view.has`). Its hierarchy is the edge `:subTaskClassOf` (new `owl:ObjectProperty` in `orchestration-v1.ttl`, domain and range `:TaskClass`), written as a graph edge with `relationship` `subTaskClassOf` from the child class node to its parent; the ontology term count rises by one property (4 triples). The closure of `descendant` match is the set of nodes from which `task_class` is reachable over `subTaskClassOf` edges, plus `task_class` itself. The handler-side accessor is the `GraphView` that `sql_read_snapshot(ctx.core, ctx.caller, ctx.rls)` returns (`crates/medulla-h-query-read/src/query/sql_read.rs:73`): `snap.graph` holds the edges and `snap.edge_properties[(source, target)]` the `relationship` of each; `QueryHandlerCtx` is unchanged and no composed-ontology class hierarchy is read (task classes are data, not ontology classes). The walk is a pure function `pub fn task_class_closure(root: &str, parent_edges: &[(String, String)]) -> BTreeSet<String>` in `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/harness_metrics.rs` (iterative, cycle-safe: a node is visited once). `taxonomy_revision`: each task-class node may carry the whole-number property `taxonomy_revision`; the taxonomy's current revision is the maximum of that property over the task-class nodes the caller can see (0 when none carries it). When `match_mode` is `descendant` and the param is present and differs from the current revision, the statement refuses (an error, not an abstention, because the read has nothing to abstain with) with `GAP_TAXONOMY_STALE: expected <param>, current <current>`; `exact` match ignores the param. The answer's `snapshot_revision` is the snapshot the closure was read at. Both new codes (`GAP_TAXONOMY_STALE`, `GAP_CURSOR_INVALID`) are added to `HarnessVocabularyErrorCode` in `crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/errors.rs` and to `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/error_registry.rs`. Rows and pagination: one row per `CapabilityGap` node whose `task_class_ref` is in the closure (`exact`: equals `task_class`), ordered by node id in byte order; columns `node_id`, `capability_ref`, `task_class_ref`, `domain_ref`, `disposition`, `revision`. The cursor is keyed by node id, not by group: `GapCursor { after_node_id: String }` in `harness_metrics.rs`, text exactly the `<after_node_id>` of the last row of the previous page, so the next page holds the rows with node id strictly greater. It carries no snapshot revision, so it stays valid across writes and restarts; an empty cursor text refuses `GAP_CURSOR_INVALID`. The next cursor is returned in the row warnings as `next_cursor=<node id>` when a further row exists. Writes: none. Depends: R004.6.2. Caller chain: arbora `ask` → engine client `uql` → `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs:138`) → `handle_uql` (`crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `harness_query(&text)` → `handle_harness_metric` → new arm `handle_capability_gaps` in `crates/medulla-h-query-read/src/query/harness_metrics.rs` for the statement `capability_gaps_by_task_class`. Shape: one `HarnessQuery { name: "capability_gaps_by_task_class", groups: &[], columns: &["node_id", "capability_ref", "task_class_ref", "domain_ref", "disposition", "revision"] }` in the `HARNESS_QUERIES` table of `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/harness_metrics.rs`, `task_class_closure` and `GapCursor` beside it, the handler arm beside `handle_run_trace_usage`, one property in `orchestration-v1.ttl`, and two error codes; no new method. | Test: `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/harness_metrics.rs::tests::task_class_closure_follows_sub_task_class_edges_once` (L1) asserts the closure of a three-level chain with a cycle. Test: `tests/orchestration/test_capability_gap_served.py::test_query_two_task_classes_across_restart_and_denied_tenant` (planned, L4) writes gaps for a parent and a child task class, asserts `exact` returns one class and `descendant` both, pages with `page_size` 1 across an engine restart, asserts a stale `taxonomy_revision` refuses `GAP_TAXONOMY_STALE`, and asserts another tenant gets no rows. |
Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R004.6.2
| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.8` | **Bind active policy to reviewed repository files.** Subsystem: policy source binding. Medulla keeps active runtime policy in the graph. Normative sources are reviewed, versioned repository files. Caller chain: the activation of R012.7.3 reads the binding record before the pointer move; the review receipt arrives from the arbora approval row ARBORA-OPS-R885. Shape: the record `PolicyBinding { repository, path, commit, blob_digest, candidate_set_digest, review_receipt_digest, invariant_generation, expected_policy_revision }` in `crates/medulla-types/src/policy_document.rs`; it reuses the `PolicyCandidate` digest of R012.7.1 and names no new store. Activation (R012.7.3) refuses when the review is missing, stale, rejected or for other digests. Medulla has no writer for normative files. A source change goes through the governed write-back of SCION-GOVERNED-WRITEBACK-R006 and approval at ARBORA-OPS-R050.2.1. `ARBORA-OPS-R050.2` consumes the review receipt. Medulla rereads the committed revision and requires a new review before activation. A graph edit never rewrites a file. Recording: the review receipt of the arbora approval row is `PolicyReview { binding: PolicyBinding, author, reviewer }` (`crates/medulla-types/src/policy_document.rs`). `DecisionLog` `learn` `RecordPolicyReview { graph, review }` checks it against the `PolicyCandidate` nodes of R012.7.2 in `graph` (repository, path, commit, blob digest and candidate-set digest must be the indexed ones, every reviewed clause must be `proposed`, and the candidate-set digest must hash from the indexed clause digests) and against the invariant generation in force, stores it once under `policy-review:<review_receipt_digest>` and answers the `ReviewApproval` evidence of `GovernedSet` `policy:<repository>`. `invariant_generation` is `sha256:` over the sorted lines `<invariant iri>\t<forbidden rule>\t<class>` of the published invariants joined by `\n`, so any client can compute it; `expected_policy_revision` is the revision ref the active policy had, absent when none was active. A review that is for other digests refuses `POLICY_REVIEW_MISMATCH`, a source indexed again or a moved invariant generation refuses `POLICY_REVIEW_STALE`, an inactive clause refuses `POLICY_CLAUSE_UNSUPPORTED`, and the same receipt digest recorded with another review refuses `POLICY_REVIEW_MISMATCH`. A rejected review is never recorded, so it reads as `POLICY_REVIEW_MISSING`. Refusal: `POLICY_REVIEW_MISSING`, `POLICY_REVIEW_MISMATCH`, `POLICY_REVIEW_STALE`, `POLICY_CLAUSE_UNSUPPORTED` (table `medulla_types::policy_document::REFUSALS`). Depends: R012.2, MEDULLA-IDENTITY-R008.4. | Test: `tests/orchestration/test_policy_activation_served.py::test_graph_mutation_leaves_source_bytes_unchanged` (L4). Test: `tests/orchestration/test_policy_activation_served.py::test_missing_or_modified_review_preserves_prior_policy` (L4). |
Depends: MEDULLA-IDENTITY-R008.4, ARBORA-OPS-R885, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R012.2

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R009.100` | **Correlated trace export read.** Subsystem: `:RunTrace` readback. Caller chain: arbora `runs.events` (ARBORA-OPS-R021.4.7) → engine client `run_trace_export` → `Method::RunTraceExport` → `export_page` in new `crates/medulla-h-query-read/src/query/run_trace_export.rs`. The request binds `root_run_id`, `time_range`, `source_revision`, `redaction_profile`, `page_size` and `page_token`. The page returns the correlated records (parent run, child agent, span, tool call), `complete` and `snapshot_digest`. `complete` is true only when every span the export declared is present in the page; a missing child makes `complete` false with `EXPORT_CHILD_MISSING`, never a silent gap. `snapshot_digest` is SHA-256 over the ordered record refs of the pinned `source_revision`. `redaction_profile` is closed: `digests_only` (default), `strip_content`, `none`. `none` needs the trace-owner principal. Page size is bounded by `orchestration_limits.page_size_max`. Refusal: `EXPORT_CHILD_MISSING`, `EXPORT_SPAN_DUPLICATE`, `EXPORT_CURSOR_STALE`, `RUN_TRACE_NOT_FOUND`. A foreign tenant reads as absent (`RUN_TRACE_NOT_FOUND`). The export reads the root `:RunTrace` node and follows exactly the three child edges the ontology declares (`:hasChildRun` to a child run, `:hasSpan`, `:hasToolCall`) and no other edge; a run lists the span ids it produced in `:declaredSpans`. | `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R010.100` | **Join an evolution proposal to its measured source traces as a read view.** Subsystem: harness metrics over `:RunTrace`. Stores: everything the join reads is a node of the caller's tenant graph, read from one RLS-filtered snapshot. (1) The proposal progress record is a node with `node_type = "EvolutionProgress"`, written by the harness through the ordinary governed write (MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R064); it holds the proposal identity, the prompt identity, the metric window and the ordered source run ids. No `:RunTrace` property names a proposal: a trace joins by its node id, which equals the run id the progress record lists. (2) The source traces are the `RunTrace` nodes of MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R009.15. (3) The prompt revision is the `ProgramRevision` node that the program promotion commit writes (`program_promotion_methods`, `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation_batch/commit/program.rs:144`, properties `type = "ProgramRevision"`, `program_ref`, `revision`, `content_digest`); the prompt version id is its `program_ref`. Vocabulary: add to `crates/medulla-core/ontology/orchestration-v1.ttl` the class `:EvolutionProgress` (`rdfs:subClassOf prov:Entity`) and the datatype properties, each with `rdfs:domain :EvolutionProgress`: `:proposalId` (xsd:string), `:proposalRevision` (xsd:integer), `:promptVersionId` (xsd:string), `:promptRevision` (xsd:integer), `:metricWindowId` (xsd:string), `:metricWindowRevision` (xsd:integer), `:sourceRunIds` (xsd:string, a JSON array of run ids in run order) and `:observedAtMs` (xsd:integer). Types: new `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/evolution_metrics.rs` (declared in `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/lib.rs`, beside `harness_metrics.rs`). `pub const EVOLUTION_TRACE_METRICS: &str = "evolution_trace_metrics";`. `pub const EVOLUTION_WINDOW_DIGEST_DOMAIN: &[u8] = b"eg/evolution-metric-window/v1";`. `pub const EVOLUTION_COLUMNS: [&str; 3] = ["value", "numerator", "denominator"];`. `EvolutionTraceQuery { proposal_id: String, proposal_revision: u64, prompt_version_id: String, prompt_revision: u64, prompt_digest: Digest256, metric_window_id: String, metric_window_revision: u64, metric_window_digest: Digest256 }` with `from_params(text: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String>, number: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<f64>) -> Result<Self, String>`, reading the UQL parameters of the same names; a missing parameter, a non-integer or negative revision, or a digest that `Digest256::parse` refuses returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT: $<name> ...`. The tenant is never a parameter: it is the verified caller's. `EvolutionProgress { proposal_id, proposal_revision: u64, prompt_version_id, prompt_revision: u64, metric_window_id, metric_window_revision: u64, source_run_ids: Vec<String>, observed_at_ms: u64 }` with `from_node(props: &Map<String, Value>) -> Option<Self>`, which reads the eight properties above and returns `None` when `node_type` is not `EvolutionProgress` or any property is missing or ill-typed. `AnswerState { Complete, Partial }` and `MetricState { Measured, Unknown }` are `Copy` classification enums computed once by the constructor and never moved, so they get no machine; so is `TraceState { Found, Missing }`. `SourceTrace { run_id: String, state: TraceState }` is one source run id and whether its trace was found. `EvolutionMetric { name: &'static str, unit: &'static str, value: Option<f64>, numerator: Option<f64>, denominator: Option<f64>, state: MetricState }`. `EvolutionTraceAnswer { sources: Vec<SourceTrace>, window_digest: Digest256, coverage: Option<f64>, state: AnswerState, observed_at_ms: u64, metrics: Vec<EvolutionMetric> }`. Window digest: `window_digest(progress: &EvolutionProgress, prompt_digest: &Digest256) -> Digest256` is `Digest256::framed(EVOLUTION_WINDOW_DIGEST_DOMAIN, fields)` with `fields` in this order: `proposal_id` UTF-8, `proposal_revision.to_be_bytes()`, `prompt_version_id` UTF-8, `prompt_revision.to_be_bytes()`, `prompt_digest.as_bytes()` (32 raw bytes), `metric_window_id` UTF-8, `metric_window_revision.to_be_bytes()`, then each source run id UTF-8 in stored order. Behaviour: `pub fn evolution_trace_metrics(query: &EvolutionTraceQuery, nodes: &BTreeMap<String, Map<String, Value>>) -> Result<EvolutionTraceAnswer, String>` over the snapshot's node id to property map. Checks, in order, each refusing and writing nothing: no `EvolutionProgress` node with `proposalId == proposal_id` and `proposalRevision == proposal_revision` refuses `EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN: proposal <id> revision <n>`; a progress node whose `promptVersionId` or `promptRevision` differs from the query refuses `EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN: prompt <id> revision <n>`; no `ProgramRevision` node with `program_ref == prompt_version_id` and `revision == prompt_revision` refuses `EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN: prompt <id> revision <n>`; a `ProgramRevision` whose `content_digest` is not `prompt_digest` (both as 64 lowercase hex) refuses `EVOLUTION_DIGEST_MISMATCH: prompt`; a progress `metricWindowId` other than `metric_window_id`, or a stored window revision lower than the query's, refuses `EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN: window <id> revision <n>`; a stored window revision higher than the query's refuses `EVOLUTION_WINDOW_STALE: window <id> is at revision <stored>`; a `window_digest` other than `metric_window_digest` refuses `EVOLUTION_DIGEST_MISMATCH: window`. Then `sources` holds one `SourceTrace` per source run id in stored run order: `Found` when the run id's node is a `RunTrace`, else `Missing`; the two lists `trace_refs` and `missing_ids` are deleted (hard cutover), so the interleaved run order is kept. `coverage` is the found count over `sources.len()` and `None` for an empty list; `state` is `Complete` only when no source is `Missing` and the list is not empty, else `Partial`. A missing trace is never counted as zero. The metrics, in this order and all with unit `ratio`, are computed over the found traces by the existing `answer_group` of `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/harness_metrics.rs` (made `pub(crate)`): `success_rate` from `harness_success_rate` (value, numerator solved, denominator found); `overflow_rate` from `harness_overflow_rate` (value, numerator overflow, denominator found); `mean_peak_ratio` from `harness_mean_peak_ratio` (value, numerator included, denominator found). A metric is `Measured` when its value is `Some`, else `Unknown` with all three channels `None`. Wire: `EvolutionTraceAnswer::to_rows(&self) -> Vec<UqlRow>` and `EvolutionTraceAnswer::from_rows(&[UqlRow]) -> Result<Self, String>` encode one `UqlResult::Rows` with columns `EVOLUTION_COLUMNS`: first one row per entry of `sources` in that order, `id = "trace:<run id>"`, channels `[None, None, None]`, provenance map `kind = trace` and `state` = `found` or `missing`; `from_rows` rebuilds `sources` in row order; then one row per metric, `id = "metric:<name>"`, channels `[value, numerator, denominator]`, provenance map `kind = metric`, `unit` and `state` = `measured` or `unknown`; last one row `id = "answer"`, channels `[coverage, found count, source count]`, provenance map `kind = answer`, `state` = `complete` or `partial`, `window_digest` (64 lowercase hex) and `observed_at_ms` (decimal). Statement: add `HarnessQuery { name: EVOLUTION_TRACE_METRICS, groups: &[], columns: &EVOLUTION_COLUMNS }` to `HARNESS_QUERIES`, so `harness_query` names it and `handle_uql` routes it. In `crates/medulla-h-query-read/src/query/harness_metrics.rs`, `handle_harness_metric` returns `handle_evolution_trace_metrics(ctx, query, params)` for that name, the mirror of `handle_run_trace_usage`: decode the query with `text_param` and `number_param`, read one `sql_read_snapshot`, collect every `node_row_object` keyed by node id, call `evolution_trace_metrics`, and return `to_rows()` with the snapshot revision; a refusal returns `Response::err(ctx.req_id, refusal)`. Registration: add `EvolutionDigestMismatch => "EVOLUTION_DIGEST_MISMATCH"`, `EvolutionRevisionUnknown => "EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN"` and `EvolutionWindowStale => "EVOLUTION_WINDOW_STALE"` in alphabetical position to `ServerErrorCode` (`crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs`), and append the three codes to the `Uql` entry of `EXACT_METHOD_ERRORS` in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs` that holds `USAGE_QUERY_INVALID`. `INVALID_ARGUMENT` is already a shared code. Writes: none; Medulla never advances Meristem state. A foreign tenant's nodes are outside the RLS snapshot, so its proposal reads as `EVOLUTION_REVISION_UNKNOWN`. Consumer: arbora `evolution.proposals.metrics` (ARBORA-OPS-R059) and `OVERSTORY-APPS-R004.7`. Depends: R009.15. Caller chain: arbora `evolution.proposals.metrics` (ARBORA-OPS-R059) → engine client `uql` with the statement text `evolution_trace_metrics` → `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs:138`) → `handle_uql` (`crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `harness_query` (`uql_statement.rs:45`) → `handle_harness_metric` → `handle_evolution_trace_metrics` → `evolution_trace_metrics`. Shape: one statement in the `HARNESS_QUERIES` table, its pure join in new `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/evolution_metrics.rs` beside `harness_metrics.rs`, one handler beside `handle_run_trace_usage`, and the `:EvolutionProgress` terms in `orchestration-v1.ttl`; it reuses `answer_group`, `sql_read_snapshot`, `Digest256::framed` and the statement runner behind `Method::Uql`, and adds no method. Budget: one snapshot scan, linear in nodes, as `run_trace_usage`; a nightly bench `benches/evolution_trace_metrics.rs` (root `benches/`, registered as `[[bench]] name = "evolution_trace_metrics"`, `path = "benches/evolution_trace_metrics.rs"`, `harness = false` in the root `Cargo.toml` beside `usage_totals`), bench group `harness/evolution_trace_metrics`, which builds a `BTreeMap<String, Map<String, Value>>` of 1,000,000 nodes (one `EvolutionProgress` with 1,000 source run ids, one `ProgramRevision`, 999 `RunTrace` nodes and filler nodes) and times one `evolution_trace_metrics` call; it never gates a PR. | Test: `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/evolution_metrics.rs::tests::answer_round_trips_through_rows` (L1) asserts `from_rows(to_rows(a)) == a` for interleaved found and missing sources, the trace rows in run order, and the window digest bytes. Test: `crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/evolution_metrics.rs::tests::missing_trace_is_partial_and_each_refusal_names_its_code` (L1). Test: `tests/orchestration/test_evolution_metrics_served.py::test_missing_trace_is_partial_not_zero` (L4) adds a `ProgramRevision`-shaped node, an `EvolutionProgress` node and one of its two `RunTrace` nodes through `AddNode`, reads the statement through `Method::Uql`, and asserts `partial`, one missing id and a stale-window refusal. |

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R750.752` | **Refusal: organization and market faults.** Subsystem: organization runtime. Four faults, each leaving state unchanged. (1) Twin with no pin: `AgentTwin` decodes through `AgentTwinWire` and `AgentTwin::new` (`crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/org.rs:73`), which refuses `TWIN_PIN_MISSING` (the `org.rs:54` constant) when every pin is blank. In the `recruit` family (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/family/recruit.rs`) `constraints` decodes the `recruit` parameter with `json_param::<RecruitRequest>`, so the served answer is the request refusal `PARAMETER_INVALID` whose detail contains `TWIN_PIN_MISSING`; `TWIN_PIN_MISSING` is a detail token, not a wire code, and nothing is registered for it. (2) Excluded offer: not a refusal. `allocate_offer` keeps only the legal offers of `derive_legal_offers` (`crates/medulla-work-types/src/work_market/selection.rs`); the answer's `RecruitResult.allocation: OfferSelection` lists every excluded gap with its `OfferExclusion` code, and its top offer is `None` when every offer is excluded, so no allocation is made from an excluded offer. (3) No transition: `op_send_event` (`crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/statechart.rs:211`) today answers a no-op when `outcome.outcome.no_op_reason == Some(NoOpReason::NoTransitionDefined)` (`crates/medulla-statechart/src/transition.rs:49`). It must instead return `Err("STATECHART_NO_TRANSITION: instance '<instance_id>' in state '<state>' has no transition on event '<event>'")` before the stored instance changes: the check runs on the `StepOutcome` computed by `StatechartStore::send_event` before it persists, so `send_event` gains the rule that a `NoTransitionDefined` outcome is returned as `StatechartError::InvalidTransition { instance_id, reason }` without a write, and `op_send_event` maps that error to the coded text. `NoOpReason::AllGuardsFalse` stays a no-op answer. This applies to every chart: the agent-instance and spec-proposal charts already document this refusal (`crates/medulla-store/src/loop_statechart.rs:320,355`). Behaviour change: a `Statechart` `SendEvent` on an event with no defined transition used to answer success with `fired: false` and now answers the refusal `STATECHART_NO_TRANSITION`; a client that read `fired: false` as the signal for an undefined transition reads the coded refusal instead (a guard that is false still answers `fired: false`). The in-process callers `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/{belief_revise,claims,feature_ledger,loops}.rs` already turn a not-fired outcome into their own refusal, so each now surfaces the store's `StatechartError::InvalidTransition` text for an undefined transition and keeps its own text for a false guard. (4) Unregistered definition: `op_instantiate` (`statechart.rs:174`) maps `StatechartError::NotFound(_)` from `instantiate_batch` (which reads `get_def(def_id)`) to `Err("INSTANCE_DEFINITION_UNREGISTERED: statechart definition '<def_id>' is not registered")`. Registration: add `StatechartNoTransition => "STATECHART_NO_TRANSITION"` and `InstanceDefinitionUnregistered => "INSTANCE_DEFINITION_UNREGISTERED"` in alphabetical position to `ServerErrorCode` (`crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs`), and add the entry `("Statechart", &["INSTANCE_DEFINITION_UNREGISTERED", "STATECHART_NO_TRANSITION"])` to `EXACT_METHOD_ERRORS` in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Depends: R750.751. Caller chain: arbora `medulla.decide.Decide` → `Method::Decide` → `RecruitFamily::constraints` for (1) and `RecruitFamily::conclude` → `recruit` → `allocate_offer` for (2); served `Method::Statechart` → `medulla_h_jobs::statechart::handle` → `op_send_event` → `StatechartStore::send_event` for (3) and `op_instantiate` → `StatechartStore::instantiate_batch` for (4). Shape: the two mappings in `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/statechart.rs`, the no-write refusal in `crates/medulla-statechart/src/store.rs`, and two registered codes; no new code set for organization or market faults. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/statechart.rs::tests::undefined_transition_and_unregistered_definition_refuse` (L1) asserts both coded texts and that the instance version is unchanged. Test: `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/stat_tests/org_runtime_tests.rs::pinless_twin_refuses_and_excluded_offer_has_no_top` (L3, async, beside `recruit_allocate_and_twin_replay`, built on its `Harness`, `publish_route_schema`, `asked` and `request` helpers) takes `serde_json::to_value(asked(None))`, sets `"twin"` to `{"agent_ref": "agent:a", "pinned_revisions": [""]}`, sends it as the `recruit` param text through `decide` and asserts the refusal is `PARAMETER_INVALID` whose detail contains `TWIN_PIN_MISSING` (faults 1); a second call over `asked(None)` with a market whose every gap is excluded asserts `allocation.top()` is `None` and each excluded gap keeps its `OfferExclusion` (fault 2). `tests/orchestration/test_org_runtime_served.py::test_unregistered_definition_refuses_with_code` (L4) calls `client.statechart.instantiate("def:r750-unregistered")` on a connected `MedullaClient` (the pattern of `tests/orchestration/test_transition_refused_served.py`) and asserts the error text starts `INSTANCE_DEFINITION_UNREGISTERED` (fault 4). The recruit wire request needs no Python builder: faults 1 and 2 are proven in Rust, where the request exists. |

| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R800.2` | **Behaviour: typed rendering (pattern selection retired).** Subsystem: definitions. Code: `render_template(template: &PromptTemplate, body: &str, values: &[(String, SlotValue)]) -> Result<String, DefinitionRefusal>` in `crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/definitions.rs`. `SlotValue` is a new enum in the same file: `Text(String)`, `Integer(i64)`, `Iri(String)`, `List(Vec<String>)`, matching `SlotType` one to one. `body` is the template text the caller read from the `body` property of the template's graph node (R800.3 reads it; the node is keyed by `PromptTemplate.id`); `PromptTemplate` holds no body. A placeholder in the body is `{{name}}`. Rule: every placeholder must name a declared slot and have exactly one value of that slot's type, else `TEMPLATE_SLOT_UNFILLED` (a declared slot with no value) or `TEMPLATE_SLOT_TYPE` (a value of the wrong type), both are variants of the `DefinitionRefusal` enum that this row owns (all four codes of R800.4 are declared with it in `definitions.rs` and registered in `declared_error_code` of `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/error_registry.rs`; this row raises only the two `TEMPLATE_SLOT_*` codes, R800.4 raises the other two). A placeholder that names no declared slot is `TEMPLATE_SLOT_UNFILLED`; a value for a slot the template does not declare, or a second value for one slot, is `TEMPLATE_SLOT_TYPE`. A `List` renders as its items joined by `, `; an `Integer` in base 10; `Text` and `Iri` verbatim. Values are never re-scanned for placeholders. It runs in O(body length + value length). Rendering never refuses for size: the 4 KiB allocation figure is the nightly budget of R800.6, not a refusal, and the template body is bounded where it is stored (R800.3). Writes: none. Pattern selection is RETIRED: there is no `pattern_select` family, no `knob pattern.min_outcomes` and no pattern-outcome store. Nothing consumes a selection (assembly reads the patterns the request names, R800.3), no outcome record names a pattern (`RunTraceRecord` carries a task signature, not a pattern), and ranking a declared option set is the existing `Decide` `QuestionKind::Rank` with `CandidateSource::Declared`; a second family would be a second route. Caller chain: served `AgentAssemble` → `handle_agent_assemble` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/assemble.rs:25`) → `assemble_for` (:46) → `render_template` (wired by R800.3). Depends: R800.1 (this row precedes R800.3 and R800.4). Shape: `render_template` and `SlotValue` in `crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/definitions.rs`; reuses `PromptTemplate` of R800.1; the refusal enum is R800.4. Class: BUILD. | Test: `crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/definitions.rs::tests::render_fills_typed_slots_only` (L1) asserts a rendered fixture of all four slot types, an unfilled slot and a wrong-typed value, and that a value containing `{{x}}` is kept verbatim. |
Depends: MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R800.1
| `MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R800.3` | **Wiring: definitions are read by assembly and revised by version.** Assembly reads the prompt templates and workflow patterns the request names and renders each assigned prompt; publishing stays `AgentLibraryStore::publish`. Build order: R800.2, then R800.3, then R800.4. Request fields (`crates/medulla-decision-types/src/decision/request.rs`): `AssemblyRequest` gains `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BoundedVec::is_empty")] pub patterns: BoundedVec<String, 8>` (WorkflowPattern node ids, `workflow:<process_iri>:v<n>`) and `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BoundedVec::is_empty")] pub prompts: BoundedVec<PromptAssignment, 8>`, with `PromptAssignment { agent_slot: u32, template_id: String, values: BoundedVec<SlotAssignment, 32> }` and `SlotAssignment { name: String, value: SlotValue }`, both `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`. `agent_slot` is the index into `AssemblyResult.agents` (0 for the one-agent graph). `SlotValue` (`crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/definitions.rs`) gains `Clone`, `Serialize` and `Deserialize` (`#[serde(tag = "type", content = "value", rename_all = "snake_case")]`) and the `schemars` derive under `contract-schema`, as `SlotType` has. Both new fields are absent from the wire when empty, so a request without them keeps its bytes. Result field: `AssemblyResult` gains `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BoundedVec::is_empty")] pub rendered_prompts: BoundedVec<RenderedPrompt, 8>` with `RenderedPrompt { agent_slot: u32, template_id: String, template_digest: String, text: String, render_digest: String }`. `AgentGraphDraft` and `AgentLibraryEntryDraft` are unchanged: they hold no raw prompt text by design (the agent pins its `system_prompt` as a `ComponentDependency`), so the rendered text travels beside the draft, never inside the durable identity. Where the bodies are read from: the `PromptTemplate` node `prompttemplate:<template_id>` of the request graph, with string properties `revision_ref`, `slots` (JSON array of `{name, slot_type}`), `digest` and `body` (the template text, at most 16384 bytes; a longer body is refused at write time with `PARAMETER_INVALID`). `Method::ProcessCompile` and `Method::ProcessModelImport` gain `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] preconditions: Vec<String>` (wire change accepted: `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method`, `medulla/generated/models.py`, the client methods `process_compile` and `process_model_import`; stored on the workflow node as the JSON property `preconditions`), and `read_definitions` lives in new `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/definitions_read.rs`. The `WorkflowPattern` facts are the nodes `workflow:<process_iri>:v<n>` that `ProcessCompile` (R700.3) writes. Node mapping (the read is validated here, nothing is returned): a stored `workflow:<process_iri>:v<n>` node maps to `WorkflowPattern` as `workflow.workflow_id` = its `process_iri` property, `workflow.version` = the decimal text of its `version` property, `workflow.content_digest` = its `workflowDigest` property (it must be `sha256:` and 64 hex digits, else `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID: definitions: <id> digest malformed`; the compile and import writers store `workflowDigest` in that one form on the node, on the `compiledFromProcess` edge and in the `ProcessCompiled` and `ProcessModelImported` answers, as `CanonicalWorkflow::digest` (`workflow_digest_of_hex` in `medulla_types::process_import`), and the reader accepts no bare 64-hex form: hard cutover), `steps` = its `steps` property, `version` = its `version` property, `process_revision` = its `processRevision` property (the compile writer stores it on the node and on the `compiledFromProcess` edge; a node written by `ProcessModelImport` has none and maps to the empty string) and `preconditions` = its `preconditions` property decoded as the JSON text of a `Vec<PatternPrecondition>` (absent decodes to the empty list; undecodable refuses `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID: definitions: <id> preconditions invalid`). Storing preconditions (new, because none are stored today): `Method::ProcessCompile` and `Method::ProcessModelImport` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs`) gain `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] preconditions: Vec<PatternPrecondition>` (at most 32 entries, each iri at most 512 chars, larger refuses `PARAMETER_INVALID`); `workflow_node` in `crates/medulla-h-graph-gateway/src/graph_ops/process_compile.rs` writes it as the string property `preconditions` (the JSON text of the list, `[]` when empty), the property is not part of `workflowDigest`, and a recompile that changes only preconditions still writes version `n + 1`. Mirror the field in `medulla/generated/models.py` and the Python client methods.  New file `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/definitions_read.rs` (module declared in `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/mod.rs`) with `pub(super) async fn read_definitions(state: &SharedState, graph: &str, verified: &VerifiedRequestContext, patterns: &[String], prompts: &[PromptAssignment]) -> Result<ReadDefinitions, String>` and `pub(super) struct ReadDefinitions { pub patterns: Vec<WorkflowPattern>, pub templates: Vec<(PromptTemplate, String)> }` (template and body). It reads through `topology_read::graph_core` behind the ordinary read ACL, like `steps_read::read`, and reads nothing when both lists are empty. Refusals: a pattern or template id that resolves to no node refuses `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID: definitions: <id> not found`; a body whose SHA-256 differs from the node's `digest` refuses `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID: definitions: <id> digest mismatch`; a pattern whose `steps` list names an id absent from the step ids of its stored `canonical` workflow refuses `DefinitionRefusal::PatternStepUnknown`. Wiring: `assemble_for` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/assemble.rs:46`) calls `read_definitions` after `steps_read::read` and, after `assemble(inputs, identity)` returns, calls `render_template` once per `PromptAssignment` and pushes one `RenderedPrompt` per assignment into `AssemblyResult.rendered_prompts` (`render_digest` is the pure function `render_digest(template_digest: &str, values: &[(String, SlotValue)]) -> String` defined by this row in `crates/medulla-work-types/src/orchestration/definitions.rs`: the lowercase hex SHA-256 of the template digest, one `\n`, and the canonical JSON (keys sorted, no whitespace) of the values sorted by name; R800.5 only adds the golden file and the premises and consumes this function). An `agent_slot` outside `assembly.agents` refuses `ASSEMBLY_INPUTS_INVALID: prompts: slot <n> is not assembled`; a render refusal returns its `TEMPLATE_SLOT_*` code as `CODE: detail`. The patterns are read and validated here and returned in no field: nothing downstream consumes them yet. Revision: publishing is `AgentLibraryStore::publish`. An edit creates a new revision at stage `Published` and never changes a published one; the head compare-and-set refuses a stale or reused revision with `STALE_AGENT_LIBRARY_REVISION` before any revision row is written, so a reused published revision needs no code of its own (the storage-level "already stored" check in `agent_library.rs` is an invariant, unreachable over the wire). The current revision is the champion pointer of R805 (`Definition` or `Program`, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.10). Writes: new revisions only. Depends: R800.2, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.10, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R036. Caller chain: served `AgentAssemble` → `handle_agent_assemble` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/assemble.rs:25`) → `assemble_for` (:46) → `read_definitions` → `render_template` per assigned prompt. Shape: one new file, three request/result types and two new fields, one derive change, one error-text change; no new store and no new method. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/definitions_read.rs::tests::pattern_with_unknown_step_refuses` (L2). Test: `tests/orchestration/test_definitions_served.py::test_edit_creates_new_revision_and_champion_selects_current` (L4). |
Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805.10, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R036, MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R800.2

The family reads the `RunObservationWindow` nodes that the producer writes to the graph; it no longer reads the usage ledger. Producer: `meristem.graph.execute_graph` run loop → `meristem/graph/budget.py` → MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R071 sends each window through `add_node` (admitted by `validate_run_observation`, R009.18.2; the stored node has `node_type` `RunObservationWindow`, the property `run_trace_ref` = the run id, and `error_rate` as `Measured`/`Unknown`). Reader: new `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/windows_read.rs` with `pub(super) async fn read_windows(state: &SharedState, graph: &str, verified: &VerifiedRequestContext, run_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<RunObservationWindow>, String>`; it reads through `topology_read::graph_core` behind the ordinary read ACL like `steps_read::read`, keeps the nodes whose `run_trace_ref` equals `run_id`, keeps only the highest `window_revision` of each `window_id`, and returns them newest first by `end_ms`. A pure `fn select_windows(windows: Vec<RunObservationWindow>, now_ms: u64, knobs: &WidthKnobs) -> Vec<RunObservationWindow>` in the same file drops windows whose `end_ms` is older than `now_ms - width.window_max_age_s * 1000` and keeps at most `width.window` of them. The serving path (`Method::Decide` for `QuestionKind::RunAdjustment`) calls `read_windows` before the family runs and passes the result as the new field `observation_windows: Option<&'a [RunObservationWindow]>` of `ExecutionContext` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/stat_executor.rs:46`), the pattern `assistant_profile` already uses; the family never reads the graph itself. Family changes (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/family/run_adjustment.rs` and `crates/medulla-decision-types/src/decision/run_adjustment.rs`): the params `child_id` and `operation` are removed and `run_id` (text, required) replaces them; `ErrorWindow` drops `child_id` and `operation` and gains `run_id: String`, `window_id: String` and `window_revision: u64` (those of the newest window); an empty `run_id` raises the existing `ERRORS_UNPARTITIONED` refusal. `recent_windows` and its `recent_run_windows` ledger call are deleted. A window counts as one run: it is errored when its `error_count` is `Measured` and above zero; its latency is the p95 of `latency_samples_ms` when `Measured`; the predicted cost is the mean of `cost.amount_minor` times 10000 (one minor unit is 10000 micro-units) over windows whose `cost` is `Measured`. The two new abstention codes `WINDOW_STALE` (every window of the run is older than the age bound; a run with no window at all abstains `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES`, since no stored-run probe exists) and `RATE_UNKNOWN` (the newest window's `error_rate` is `Unknown`, so no rate exists) are constants beside `LATENCY_UNKNOWN`, are added to `REFUSALS`, and are raised as `AbstainReason::Family { family: QuestionKind::RunAdjustment, code, detail }`; neither is zero evidence. The code of a mid-run widening is `WIDEN_MID_RUN` (as built), not `WIDEN_MID_RUN_REFUSED`; it stays a `PARAMETER_INVALID` refusal. Persisted record: the family writes nothing. The adjustment record is the sealed `DecisionRecord` of the answer, which Meristem makes durable through `DecisionCommit` and applies only after the commit is acknowledged (MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R071); the family adds the premises `run_adjustment.window_id=<id>`, `run_adjustment.window_revision=<n>` (class `Observation`, source the window node id) so the record names the window it read. A mid-run widening request is refused as above; a later-run allocation needs a separate capacity grant from `ARBORA-CAPACITY-R001.1`. Depends: R009.18.2, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R158.7, MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R071. Caller chain: producer as above → `add_node` → `direct_add_node` (R009.18.2); consumer: served `Decide` kind `run_adjustment` → `read_windows` → `RunAdjustmentFamily::conclude` (`crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/family/run_adjustment.rs`) → `decide_adjustment`. Shape: one new file, one `ExecutionContext` field, one param change, two codes; the capacity grant stays with `ARBORA-CAPACITY-R001.1`. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/windows_read.rs::tests::newest_revision_per_window_inside_age_bound` (L2). Test: `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/stat_tests/run_adjustment_tests.rs::windows_drive_model_and_width_and_refuse_mid_run_widening` (L3).
